\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\nabla\mu_{k}\cdot\nabla|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=\int_{\Omega}\mu_{k}\nabla\phi_{k}\cdot\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}\cdot\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\chi(L)}{2}\Big(\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\Big)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\theta_{k}-\mu_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma.

Then, using integration by parts, we observe that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\Big(\partial_{t}\rho(\phi_{k})+\textnormal{div}(\rho(\phi_{k})\mathbf{v}_{\ast})\Big)|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\Big(\partial_{t}\sigma(\psi_{k})+\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(\sigma(\psi_{k})\mathbf{w}_{\ast})\Big)|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Omega}\textnormal{div}(m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\nabla\mu_{k})|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\Big(\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})-\beta m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{k}\Big)|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=\int_{\Omega}\mu_{k}\nabla\phi_{k}\cdot\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}\cdot\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma.

Here, we have additionally employed the assumption
\beta(\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1})=\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}
in the case
L=0
to rewrite the boundary contribution arising from the integration by parts as

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{k}|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma=\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{k}|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma.

Then, as
\mathbf{v}_{\ast}
and
\mathbf{w}_{\ast}
are both divergence-free on their respective domains, we find that

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\Big(\partial_{t}\rho(\phi_{k})+\textnormal{div}(\rho(\phi_{k})\mathbf{v}_{\ast})\Big)|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\Big(\partial_{t}\sigma(\psi_{k})+\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(\sigma(\psi_{k})\mathbf{w}_{\ast})\Big)|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Omega}\Big(\partial_{t}\phi_{k}+\mathbf{v}_{\ast}\cdot\nabla\phi_{k}\Big)|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\Big(\partial_{t}\psi_{k}+\mathbf{w}_{\ast}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi_{k})\Big)|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Omega}\textnormal{div}(m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\nabla\mu_{k})|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\Big(\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})-\beta m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{k}\Big)|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma,

see also (
1.5
).
Thus, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

(4.16)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=-\int_{\Omega}\phi_{k}\nabla\mu_{k}\cdot\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\cdot\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma.

For the right-hand side, we use

\displaystyle\Big|-\int_{\Omega}\phi_{k}\nabla\mu_{k}\cdot\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\cdot\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq\|\nabla\mu_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\|\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\|\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq\frac{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}^{2},

which leads to the differential inequality

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Big)+\frac{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq\frac{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}^{2}.

Integrating the above inequality over
[0,s]
for some
s\in[0,T]
, and using (
4.11
), it follows that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{\rho_{\ast}}{2}|\mathbf{v}_{k}(s)|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\frac{\sigma_{\ast}}{2}|\mathbf{w}_{k}(s)|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq\int_{\Omega}\frac{\rho^{\ast}}{2}|\mathbf{P}_{\mathbfcal{V}_{k}}^{\Omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0})|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\frac{\sigma^{\ast}}{2}|\mathbf{P}_{\mathbfcal{V}_{k}}^{\Gamma}(\mathbf{w}_{0})|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\frac{1}{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}}E_{\mathrm{free}}(\phi_{0},\psi_{0})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{1}{2\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}}\int_{0}^{s}\|(\mathbf{v}_{\ast}(\tau),\mathbf{w}_{\ast}(\tau))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau,

which, in turn, entails that

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{v}_{k}(s),\mathbf{w}_{k}(s)\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq c_{\ast}\big(\rho^{\ast}+\sigma^{\ast})\|(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{2c_{\ast}}{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}}E_{\mathrm{free}}(\phi_{0},\psi_{0})

(4.17)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{c_{\ast}}{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}}\int_{0}^{s}\|(\mathbf{v}_{\ast}(\tau),\mathbf{w}_{\ast}(\tau))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau,

where
c_{\ast}\coloneqq\tfrac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\tfrac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}
. At this point, setting

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle=\rho^{\ast}c_{\ast}\big(\rho^{\ast}+\sigma^{\ast})\|(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{2c_{\ast}}{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}}E_{\mathrm{free}}(\phi_{0},\psi_{0}),

\displaystyle R_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{c_{\ast}}{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}},\hskip 20.00003ptR_{3}=R_{1}T,

and assuming

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|(\mathbf{v}_{\ast}(\tau),\mathbf{w}_{\ast}(\tau))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau\leq R_{3}\mathbf{e}^{R_{2}t}\hskip 20.00003pt\text{for all~}t\in[0,T],

(4.18)

we deduce from (
4.17
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{0}^{t}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k}(s),\mathbf{w}_{k}(s))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}s\leq R_{3}+R_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s}\|(\mathbf{v}_{\ast}(\tau),\mathbf{w}_{\ast}(\tau))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau\;\mathrm{d}s\leq R_{3}\mathbf{e}^{R_{2}t}\end{split}

(4.19)

for all
t\in[0,T]
. Furthermore, we also infer that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k}(t),\mathbf{w}_{k}(t))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\leq\big(R_{1}+R_{3}R_{2}\mathbf{e}^{R_{2}T}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\eqqcolon M_{0}.

(4.20)

Next, we aim to control the time derivative
(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})
. To this end, we multiply (
4.12
) by
\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}a_{l}^{k}
and sum over
l=1,\ldots,k
. This yields

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{k})|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{k})|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=-\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{k})(\mathbf{v}_{\ast}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{k})(\mathbf{w}_{\ast}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}:\mathbf{D}\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}:\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})(\nabla\mu_{k}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})(\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\int_{\Omega}\phi_{k}\nabla\mu_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\chi(L)}{2}\Big(\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\Big)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\theta_{k}-\mu_{k})\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma.

By exploiting (
4.1
), we use Hölder’s inequality and the Sobolev embedding to find that

\displaystyle\rho_{\ast}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\sigma_{\ast}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq\rho^{\ast}\|\mathbf{v}_{\ast}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\sigma^{\ast}\|\mathbf{w}_{\ast}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{\infty}(\Gamma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+2\nu^{\ast}\|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\|\mathbf{D}\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+2\nu^{\ast}\|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\gamma^{\ast}\|\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}+\Big|\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big|m^{\ast}\|\nabla\mu_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\Big|\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}\Big|m^{\ast}\|\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{\infty}(\Gamma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\|\nabla\mu_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\|\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\tfrac{1}{2}\chi(L)\Big|\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\Big|\|\beta\theta_{k}-\mu_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{\infty}(\Gamma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq C(\rho^{\ast}+\sigma^{\ast})\|(\mathbf{v}_{\ast},\mathbf{w}_{\ast})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{W}^{2,4}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\nu^{\ast}C_{k}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}+\gamma^{\ast}\|\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

(4.21)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+Cm^{\ast}\bigg(\Big|\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big|+\Big|\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}\Big|\bigg)\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{W}^{2,4}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+C\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\chi(L)C\Big|\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\Big|\|\beta\theta-\mu_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{H}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq C_{k}(\rho^{\ast}+\sigma^{\ast})\|(\mathbf{v}_{\ast},\mathbf{w}_{\ast})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\nu^{\ast}C_{k}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}+\gamma^{\ast}\|\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+C_{k}m^{\ast}\bigg(\Big|\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big|+\Big|\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}\Big|\bigg)\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+C_{k}\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\chi(L)C_{k}\Big|\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big|\|\beta\theta_{k}-\mu_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma)}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}.

Eventually, in view of (
4.11
) and (
4.18
)-(
4.20
), we infer that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}(\tau),\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}(\tau))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq 3\Big(C_{k}(\rho^{\ast}+\sigma^{\ast})\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)M_{0}\Big)^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\|(\mathbf{v}_{\ast},\mathbf{w}_{\ast})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+3\Big(\nu^{\ast}C_{k}^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)\Big)^{2}R_{4}\mathbf{e}^{R_{2}T}+3\Big(\gamma^{\ast}\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)\Big)^{2}R_{4}\mathbf{e}^{R_{2}T}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+3\Big(C_{k}m^{\ast}\bigg(\Big|\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big|+\Big|\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}\Big|\bigg)\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)M_{0}+M_{0}\Big)^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\|(\mu_{k}(\tau),\theta_{k}(\tau))\|_{L,\beta}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\Big(C_{k}\Big|\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big|\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)M_{0}\Big)^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\|\beta\theta_{k}(\tau)-\mu_{k}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma)}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau

(4.22)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq\Bigg(3\Big(C_{k}(\rho^{\ast}+\sigma^{\ast})\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)M_{0}\Big)^{2}+3\Big(\nu^{\ast}C_{k}^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)\Big)^{2}+3\Big(\gamma^{\ast}\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)\Big)\Bigg)R_{4}\mathbf{e}^{R_{2}T}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+3\Big(C_{k}m^{\ast}\bigg(\Big|\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big|+|\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}|\bigg)\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)M_{0}+M_{0}\Big)^{2}\Big(2E_{\mathrm{free}}(\phi_{0},\psi_{0})+R_{4}\mathbf{e}^{R_{2}T}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\Big(C_{k}\Big|\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big|\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\ast}}\Big)M_{0}\Big)^{2}\Big(2E_{\mathrm{free}}(\phi_{0},\psi_{0})+R_{4}\mathbf{e}^{R_{2}T}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\eqqcolon M_{1}^{2},

where
M_{1}
only depends on
\rho_{\ast},\rho^{\ast},\sigma_{\ast},\sigma^{\ast},\gamma^{\ast},E_{\mathrm{free}}(\phi_{0},\psi_{0}),L,T,\Omega,m_{\ast},m^{\ast}
and
k
.

Now we define the setting of the fixed-point argument. We introduce the set

\displaystyle\mathbfcal{S}=\{(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\in H^{1}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k}):

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|(\mathbf{v}(\tau),\mathbf{w}(\tau))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau\leq R_{4}\mathbf{e}^{R_{2}t}\ \text{for all~}t\in[0,T],

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w})\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k})}\leq M_{1}\},

which is a subset of
L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k})
, and define the map

\displaystyle\Lambda:\mathbfcal{S}\rightarrow L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k}),\hskip 20.00003pt\Lambda(\mathbf{v}_{\ast},\mathbf{w}_{\ast})=(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k}),

where
(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})
is the solution to the system (
4.12
). By (
4.19
) and (
4
) it follows immediately that
\Lambda:\mathbfcal{S}\rightarrow\mathbfcal{S}
. We readily notice that
\mathbfcal{S}
is convex and closed. Moreover, as a subset of
L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k})
,
\mathbfcal{S}
is compact. We are left to prove the continuity of the map
\Lambda
. This is done by adapting the argument in
[
30
, Theorem 6.1]
to the case with non-degenerate mobilities by using the results established in Appendix
A
. To this end, let us consider a sequence
\{(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{n})\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\subset\mathbfcal{S}
such that
(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{n})\rightarrow(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{\ast},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{\ast})
in
L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k})
. By arguing as above, there exists a sequence
(\widehat{\phi}_{n},\widehat{\psi}_{n},\widehat{\mu}_{n},\widehat{\theta}_{n})
,
n\in\mathbb{N}
, and
(\widehat{\phi}_{\ast},\widehat{\psi}_{\ast},\widehat{\mu}_{\ast},\widehat{\theta}_{\ast})
that solve the convective bulk-surface Cahn–Hilliard equation (
4.8
), where
(\mathbf{v}_{\ast},\mathbf{w}_{\ast})
is replaced by
(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{n})
and
(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{\ast},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{\ast})
, respectively. Observing that
(\widehat{\phi}_{n}(0),\widehat{\psi}_{n}(0))=(\widehat{\phi}_{\ast}(0),\widehat{\psi}_{\ast}(0))
for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
, an application of (
A.20
) shows that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|((\widehat{\phi}_{n}-\widehat{\phi},\widehat{\psi}_{n}-\widehat{\psi}_{\ast}\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1}_{L,\beta})^{\prime}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}\|(\widehat{\phi}_{n}-\widehat{\phi}_{\ast},\widehat{\psi}_{n}-\widehat{\psi}_{\ast})\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}t\\
&\quad\leq\exp\Big(\int_{0}^{T}P_{n}\;\mathrm{d}t\Big)\int_{0}^{T}\|(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n}-\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{\ast},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{n}-\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{\ast})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}t,\end{split}

(4.23)

where

\displaystyle P_{n}(\cdot)=C\big(1+\|(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{\ast},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{\ast})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\widehat{\mu}_{\ast},\widehat{\theta}_{\ast})\|_{L,\beta}^{2}+\|(\partial_{t}\widehat{\phi}_{n},\partial_{t}\widehat{\psi}_{n})\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1}_{L,\beta})^{\prime}}^{2}+\|(\widehat{\phi}_{n},\widehat{\psi}_{n})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}^{4}\big),

and the constant
C>0
is independent of
n\in\mathbb{N}
. To pass to the limit in (
4.23
), we have to show that the exponential term is uniformly bounded in
n\in\mathbb{N}
. To do so, we first notice that, since
\{(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{n})\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
belongs to the space
\mathbfcal{S}
, it is especially uniformly bounded in
L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{L}^{2})
. Thus, invoking the estimates (
A.10
)-(
A.12
), we find a constant
C=C(p)>0
such that

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\phi}_{n},\widehat{\psi}_{n})\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;\mathcal{H}^{1})}+\|(\partial_{t}\widehat{\phi}_{n},\partial_{t}\widehat{\psi}_{n})\|_{L^{2}(0,T;(\mathcal{H}^{1}_{L,\beta})^{\prime})}+\|(\nabla\widehat{\mu}_{n},\nabla_{\Gamma}\widehat{\theta}_{n})\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{L}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\chi(L)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta\widehat{\theta}_{n}-\widehat{\mu}_{n}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Gamma))}+\|(\widehat{\phi}_{n},\widehat{\psi}_{n})\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathcal{W}^{2,p})}

(4.24)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\|(F^{\prime}(\widehat{\phi}_{n}),G^{\prime}(\widehat{\psi}_{n}))\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathcal{L}^{p})}+\|(\widehat{\phi}_{n},\widehat{\psi}_{n})\|_{L^{4}(0,T;\mathcal{H}^{2})}\leq C

for any
2\leq p<\infty
. Consequently, it follows that

\displaystyle\exp\Big(\int_{0}^{T}P_{n}(\tau)\;\mathrm{d}t\Big)\leq C.

Thus, we infer from (
4.23
) with an application of the bulk-surface Poincaré inequality that

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\phi}_{n}-\widehat{\phi}_{\ast},\widehat{\psi}_{n}-\widehat{\psi}_{\ast})\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;(\mathcal{H}^{1}_{L,\beta})^{\prime})\cap L^{2}(0,T;\mathcal{H}^{1})}\rightarrow 0

(4.25)

as
n\rightarrow\infty
.
Then, the proof of
[
29
, Theorem 3.4]
yields the estimates

\displaystyle|\textnormal{mean}(\widehat{\mu}_{n},\widehat{\theta}_{n})|\leq C\big(1+\|(\widehat{\mu}_{n},\widehat{\theta}_{n})\|_{L,\beta}\big).

(4.26)

In the original formulation in
[
29
]
, the right-hand side additionally contains a term involving the normal derivative
\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\widehat{\phi}_{n}
. However, this extra term appears only in the case
K=0
. Since we restrict our analysis to the case
K\in(0,\infty)
, this contribution does not arise and can therefore be omitted.
Consequently, on account of the bulk-surface Poincaré inequality, we infer from (
4.26
) with (
4
) that

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\mu}_{n},\widehat{\theta}_{n})\|_{L^{2}(0,T;\mathcal{H}^{1})}\leq C.

(4.27)

Then, in view of (
4
), we find that

\displaystyle(F^{\prime}(\widehat{\phi}_{n}),G^{\prime}(\widehat{\psi}_{n}))\rightarrow(F^{\prime}(\widehat{\phi}_{\ast}),G^{\prime}(\widehat{\psi}_{\ast}))\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly in~}L^{2}(0,T;\mathcal{L}^{2}).

(4.28)

Here, we have additionally used the fact that
F_{0}^{\prime}
and
G_{0}^{\prime}
are maximal monotone operators to identify the limit functions (see, e.g.,
[
30
]
for more details). Next, in view of the weak formulation (
4.7
) and the convergences (
4.25
) and (
4.28
), we obtain

\displaystyle(\widehat{\mu}_{n},\widehat{\theta}_{n})\rightarrow(\widehat{\mu}_{\ast},\widehat{\theta}_{\ast})\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly in~}L^{2}(0,T;(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\prime}).

As we additionally have the uniform bound (
4.27
), it readily follows that

\displaystyle(\widehat{\mu}_{n},\widehat{\theta}_{n})\rightarrow(\widehat{\mu}_{\ast},\widehat{\theta}_{\ast})\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly in~}L^{2}(0,T;\mathcal{H}^{1}_{L,\beta}).

Now, to show the continuity of
\Lambda
, we have to prove that

\displaystyle\Lambda(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n},\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n})\rightarrow\Lambda(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{\ast},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{\ast})\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k})

as
n\rightarrow\infty
. To this end, using the abbreviation
(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n})=\Lambda(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{n})
, we recall that by definition of
\Lambda
,
(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n})
satisfies the variational formulation

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\rho(\widehat{\phi}_{n})\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\widehat{\psi}_{n})\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}\rho(\widehat{\phi}_{n})(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\widehat{\psi}_{n})(\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{n}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\widehat{\phi}_{n})(\nabla\widehat{\mu}_{n}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}x+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\widehat{\psi}_{n})(\nabla_{\Gamma}\widehat{\theta}_{n}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

(4.29)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\widehat{\phi}_{n})\mathbf{D}\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n}:\mathbf{D}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\widehat{\psi}_{n})\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n}:\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\widehat{\phi}_{n},\widehat{\psi}_{n})\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=\int_{\Omega}\widehat{\mu}_{n}\nabla\widehat{\phi}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\widehat{\theta}_{n}\nabla_{\Gamma}\widehat{\psi}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\chi(L)}{2}\Big(\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\big)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\widehat{\theta}_{n}-\widehat{\mu}_{n})\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{l}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma,

for all
l=1,\ldots,k
. Now, we notice that, since
\{(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n})\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\subset\mathbfcal{S}
, there exists
(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{\ast},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{\ast})\in H^{1}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k})
such that

\displaystyle(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{\ast},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{\ast})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly in~}H^{1}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k}).

(4.30)

In light of the compact embedding
H^{1}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k})\hookrightarrow C([0,T];\mathbfcal{V}_{k})
and (
4.1
), we readily deduce from (
4.30
) and by compactness, along a non-relabeled subsequence, that

\displaystyle(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{\ast},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{\ast})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}C([0,T];\mathbfcal{V}_{k}),

(4.31)

\displaystyle(\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n},\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{\ast},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{\ast})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}C([0,T];\mathbfcal{L}^{2}).

(4.32)

Now, the convergences (
4.30
)-(
4.32
) allow us to pass to the limit
n\rightarrow\infty
in (
4.29
). As
(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{\ast},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{\ast})
further satisfies the initial condition
(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{\ast},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{\ast})|_{t=0}=\mathbfcal{P}_{\mathbfcal{V}_{k}}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})
, which follows readily from (
4.31
), we infer that
(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{\ast},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{\ast})
is a solution to (
4.12
)-(
4.13
). In view of the uniqueness of solutions to (
4.12
)-(
4.13
), this implies
(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{\ast},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{\ast})=\Lambda(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{\ast},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{\ast})
, which entails

\displaystyle\Lambda(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{n},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{n})=(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{n},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{n})\rightarrow(\mathbf{u}^{\Omega}_{\ast},\mathbf{u}^{\Gamma}_{\ast})=\Lambda(\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{\ast},\widehat{\mathbf{w}}_{\ast})\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{V}_{k})

as
n\rightarrow\infty
.
This proves that the map
\Lambda
is continuous.
Finally, we are in a position to apply the Schauder fixed-point theorem and conclude that the map
\Lambda
has a fixed-point in
\mathbfcal{S}
, which gives the existence of the approximate solution
(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k},\phi_{k},\psi_{k},\mu_{k},\theta_{k})
satisfying (
4.3
)-(
4.6
) for any
k\in\mathbb{N}
.

A priori Estimates for the Approximate Solutions

We first observe that, as
T>0
was arbitrarily chosen, the approximate solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k},\phi_{k},\psi_{k},\mu_{k},\theta_{k})
exist on
(0,\infty)
for any
k\in\mathbb{N}
. In the following, we denote with the letter
C
generic constants that are independent of
k\in\mathbb{N}
.
We start by establishing a priori estimates, which are based on an energy estimate for the approximate solutions. To this end, we take
(\widehat{\mathbf{v}},\widehat{\mathbf{w}})=(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})
in (
4.3
) and perform integration by parts. This yields

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

(4.33)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=-\int_{\Omega}\phi_{k}\nabla\mu_{k}\cdot\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\cdot\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma,

see (
4
). Then, taking
(\zeta,\xi)=(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})
in (
4.4
) and
(\eta,\vartheta)=-(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})
in (
4.7
), we find with integration by parts that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{2}|\nabla\phi_{k}|^{2}+F(\phi_{k})\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\frac{1}{2}|\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}|^{2}+G(\psi_{k})\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\chi(K)\int_{\Gamma}\frac{1}{2}(\alpha\psi_{k}-\phi_{k})^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\nabla\mu_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\chi(L)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\theta_{k}-\mu_{k})^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

(4.34)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=\int_{\Omega}\phi_{k}\nabla\mu_{k}\cdot\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\cdot\mathbf{w}_{k}.

By summming (
4
) and (
4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}E_{\mathrm{tot}}(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k},\phi_{k},\psi_{k})

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\nabla\mu_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\chi(L)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\theta_{k}-\mu_{k})^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma=0.

Integrating in time from
0
to
t
for any
t\in(0,\infty)
, we have

\displaystyle E_{\mathrm{tot}}(\mathbf{v}_{k}(t),\mathbf{w}_{k}(t),\phi_{k}(t),\psi_{k}(t))

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\int_{0}^{t}\Bigg(\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Bigg)\;\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\int_{0}^{t}\Bigg(\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\nabla\mu_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\;\mathrm{d}s+\chi(L)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\theta_{k}-\mu_{k})^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Bigg)\;\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=E_{\mathrm{tot}}(\mathbfcal{P}_{\mathbfcal{V}_{k}}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0}),\phi_{0},\psi_{0}).

As the initial energy can be estimated as

\displaystyle E_{\mathrm{tot}}(\mathbfcal{P}_{\mathbfcal{V}_{k}}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0}),\phi_{0},\psi_{0})\leq\frac{\max\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}}+E_{\mathrm{free}}(\phi_{0},\psi_{0}),

it follows using
|\phi_{k}|<1
a.e. in
\Omega\times(0,\infty)
,
|\psi_{k}|<1
a.e. in
\Gamma\times(0,\infty)
and the bulk-surface Korn inequality (
2.4
) that

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathbfcal{L}^{2})}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{L^{2}(0,\infty;\mathbfcal{H}^{1})}\leq C,

(4.35)

\displaystyle\|(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{H}^{1})}\leq C,

(4.36)

\displaystyle\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{L^{2}(0,\infty;\mathbfcal{L}^{2})}+\chi(L)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta\theta_{k}-\mu_{k}\|_{L^{2}(0,\infty;L^{2}(\Gamma))}\leq C.

(4.37)

Then, recalling (
4.26
), we employ the bulk-surface Poincaré inequality to deduce that

\displaystyle\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}\leq C\big(1+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}\big).

(4.38)

Then, by
[
20
, Proposition 5.5]
and another application of the bulk-surface Poincaré inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\|_{\mathcal{W}^{2,p}}+\|(F^{\prime}(\phi_{k}),G^{\prime}(\psi_{k}))\|_{\mathcal{L}^{p}}&\leq C_{p}\big(1+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}\big)\\
&\leq C_{p}\big(1+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}\big)\end{split}

(4.39)

for any
2\leq p<\infty
. By comparison in (
4.4
), it holds that

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1}_{L,\beta})^{\prime}}\leq C\big(\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}\big).

(4.40)

Here, we have additionally made use of (
4.10
) and the boundedness of the mobility functions. Lastly, noting on
K\in(0,\infty)
, it follows from
[
20
, Corollary 5.4]
in combination with (
4.36
) and (
4.38
) that

\displaystyle\|(-\Delta\phi_{k},-\Delta_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}+\alpha\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\phi_{k})\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\leq C\big(1+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}\big).

Thus, by elliptic regularity theory for systems with bulk-surface coupling, we obtain

\displaystyle\|(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}^{4}\leq C\big(1+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}^{2}\big).

(4.41)

Consequently, collecting the above estimates, we infer from (
4.35
) and (
4.37
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})\|_{L^{2}(0,\infty;(\mathcal{H}^{1}_{L,\beta})^{\prime})}+\|(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{uloc}}(0,\infty;\mathcal{W}^{2,p})}+\|(F^{\prime}(\phi_{k}),G^{\prime}(\psi_{k}))\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{uloc}}(0,\infty;\mathcal{L}^{p})}\\
&\quad+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{uloc}}(0,\infty;\mathcal{L}^{2})}+\|(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\|_{L^{4}_{\mathrm{uloc}}(0,\infty;\mathcal{H}^{2})}\leq C_{p}\end{split}

(4.42)

for any
2\leq p<\infty
.

Higher Order Estimates for the Approximate Solutions

Next, we establish higher-order estimates for the approximate solution
(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k},\phi_{k},\psi_{k},\mu_{k},\theta_{k})
. Based on the estimates (
4.35
)-(
4.37
) and (
4.42
), we can use Theorem
A.3
, which entails the uniform estimates

\displaystyle\|(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{W}^{2,p})}+\|(F^{\prime}(\phi_{k}),G^{\prime}(\psi_{k}))\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{L}^{p})}+\|(F^{\prime\prime}(\phi_{k}),G^{\prime\prime}(\psi_{k}))\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{L}^{p})}\leq K_{1,p}

(4.43)

for any
2\leq p\leq\infty
, as well as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\infty;(\mathcal{H}^{1}_{L,\beta})^{\prime})}+\|(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{uloc}}(0,\infty;\mathcal{H}^{1})}\\
&\qquad+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{H}^{1})}+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{uloc}}(0,\infty;\mathcal{H}^{3})}\leq K_{2}.\end{split}

(4.44)

Moreover, as a consequence of (
4.43
), we have

\displaystyle\|(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{H}^{3})}+\|(F^{\prime\prime\prime}(\phi_{k}),G^{\prime\prime\prime}(\psi_{k}))\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{L}^{p})}\leq K_{3,p}

(4.45)

for any
2\leq p<\infty
. Next, we aim to establish higher-order estimates of the approximate velocity fields
(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})
. To this end, we take
(\widehat{\mathbf{v}},\widehat{\mathbf{w}})=(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})
as a test function in (
4.3
) and find

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{k})|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{k})|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=-\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{k})(\mathbf{v}_{k}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{k})(\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}^{\prime}(\phi_{k})\partial_{t}\phi_{k}|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}^{\prime}(\psi_{k})\partial_{t}\psi_{k}|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Gamma}\big(\partial_{1}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\partial_{t}\phi_{k}+\partial_{2}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\partial_{t}\psi_{k}\big)|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

(4.46)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})(\nabla\mu_{k}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})(\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\chi(L)}{2}\Big(\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}\Big)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\theta_{k}-\mu_{k})\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\int_{\Omega}\phi_{k}\nabla\mu_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\eqqcolon\sum_{j=1}^{10}R_{j}.

We now intend to find suitable bounds for the terms
R_{j}
,
j=1,\ldots,10
, by utilizing the estimates (
4.43
)-(
4.45
). Let
\varpi
be a positive constant whose precise value will be determined later. Exploiting (
2.3
), (
2.4
) and (
B.21
), we find

\displaystyle\begin{split}|R_{1}+R_{2}|&\leq\max\{\rho^{\ast},\sigma^{\ast}\}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+C\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\varpi}{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+C\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{4}.\end{split}

(4.47)

Then, in view of the assumptions on the coefficients, we have by (
2.1
), (
2.3
), (
4.40
) and (
B.21
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5}|&\leq C\|\partial_{t}\phi_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}(\Omega)}^{2}+C\|\partial_{t}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}(\Gamma)}^{2}\\
&\quad+C\big(\|\partial_{t}\phi_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma)}+\|\partial_{t}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma)}\big)\|\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}(\Gamma)}^{2}\\
&\leq C\|(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{\varpi}{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+C\|(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.48)

Next, using interpolation of Sobolev spaces (see, e.g.,
[
10
, Lemma 2.2]
), (
4.38
) and (
B.21
), we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}|R_{6}+R_{7}|&\leq C\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{\infty}}\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+C\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.49)

By the trace theorem in combination with (
4.38
) we find that

\displaystyle\begin{split}|R_{8}|&\leq C\|\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{k}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}(\Gamma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\\
&\quad\leq\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+C\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.50)

Lastly, in view of (
4.10
), we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}|R_{9}+R_{10}|&\leq\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+C\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.51)

Combining the above estimates (
4.47
)-(
4.51
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Big)\\
&\qquad+\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{2}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\\
&\quad\leq\varpi\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+C\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+C\big(\|(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\big)\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2},\end{split}

(4.52)

where
C>0
is a constant that depends on
\varpi
, but is independent of
k
. We are left to control the term
\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}
. This is done by employing regularity theory for the bulk-surface Stokes system as developed in Appendix
B
. To this end, we first notice that, for any
k\in\mathbb{N}
and a.e.
t\in(0,\infty)
, there exists a pressure pair
(p_{k}(t),q_{k}(t))\in\mathcal{H}^{1}\cap\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(0)}
such that

\displaystyle\rho(\phi_{k})\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}+\rho(\phi_{k})(\mathbf{v}_{k}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{k}+(\mathbf{J}_{k}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{k}-\textnormal{div}(2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k})+\nabla p_{k}=\mu_{k}\nabla\phi_{k}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{a.e.~in~}Q

(4.53a)

\displaystyle\sigma(\psi_{k})\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}+\sigma(\psi_{k})(\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{k}+(\mathbf{K}_{k}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{k}-\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k})+\nabla_{\Gamma}q_{k}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt=\theta_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}-2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\big[\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}\,\mathbf{n}\big]_{\tau}+\tfrac{1}{2}(\mathbf{J}_{k}\cdot\mathbf{n})\mathbf{w}-\tfrac{1}{2}(\mathbf{J}_{\Gamma,k}\cdot\mathbf{n})\mathbf{w}_{k}-\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})\mathbf{w}_{k}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{a.e.~on~}\Sigma.

(4.53b)

Here, we have used the shorthand notation

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}_{k}=-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{2}-\tilde{\rho}_{1}}{2}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\nabla\mu_{k},\hskip 20.00003pt\mathbf{K}_{k}=-\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k},\hskip 20.00003pt\mathbf{J}_{\Gamma,k}=-\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{2}-\tilde{\sigma}_{1}}{2}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})\nabla\mu_{k}.

Consequently, in view of Theorem
B.2
, we find that

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\|(p_{k},q_{k})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq C\big(\|-\rho(\phi_{k})\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k}+\rho(\phi_{k})(\mathbf{v}_{k}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{k}-(\mathbf{J}_{k}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{k}+\mu_{k}\nabla\phi_{k}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\|-\sigma(\psi_{k})\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k}-\sigma(\psi_{k})(\mathbf{w}_{k}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{k}-(\mathbf{K}_{k}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{k}+\theta_{k}\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi_{k}+\tfrac{1}{2}(\mathbf{J}_{k}\cdot\mathbf{n})\mathbf{w}_{k}-\tfrac{1}{2}(\mathbf{J}_{\Gamma,k}\cdot\mathbf{n})\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}^{2}\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{\infty}}^{2}\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{k}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma)}^{2}\|\mathbf{w}_{k}\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+C\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}+\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\big).

Thus, by Young’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\|(p_{k},q_{k})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}^{2}&\leq C_{0}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+C_{0}\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}^{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}\big(\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\big)\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.54)

Let us now set

\displaystyle\varepsilon=\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{4C_{0}},\hskip 20.00003pt\varpi=\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{8C_{0}}.

Then, multiplying (
4.54
) by
\varepsilon
and summing the resulting inequality to (
4.52
), we deduce the differential inequality

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})|\mathbf{w}_{k}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Big)\\
&\qquad+\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{8C_{0}}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{4}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\min\{\rho_{\ast},\sigma_{\ast}\}}{4C_{0}}\|(p_{k},q_{k})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}^{2}\\
&\quad\leq C\|(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})\|_{L,\beta}^{2}+C\big(\|(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\big)\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.55)

Observing that

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{v}_{k}(0),\mathbf{w}_{k}(0))\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}\leq C\|(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}},

we infer from the Gronwall lemma together with (
4.35
) and (
4.44
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k}(t),\mathbf{w}_{k}(t))\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\leq C\big(1+\|(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\big).

(4.56)

To derive a global control independent of time, we use a uniform variant of the Gronwall lemma as stated in Lemma
A.5
. Since

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{t}^{t+1}\|(\partial_{t}\phi_{k},\partial_{t}\psi_{k})\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}s\leq C_{1},

(4.57)

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{t}^{t+1}\|(\nabla\mu_{k},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}s\leq C_{2},

(4.58)

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{t}^{t+1}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}s\leq C_{3},

(4.59)

an application of Lemma
A.5
with
r=1
and
t_{0}=0
yields that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 1}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k}(t),\mathbf{w}_{k}(t))\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}\leq\big(C_{3}+C_{2})\mathbf{e}^{C_{1}}.

(4.60)

Then, for any
t\geq 0
, we integrate (
4.55
) in time from
t
to
t+1
. Thanks to (
4.57
)-(
4.59
), it follows from (
4.56
) and (
4.60
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{t}^{t+1}\|(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{k},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{k})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(p_{k},q_{k})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}s\leq C.

(4.61)

Step 4: Passage to the Limit and Existence of global Strong Solutions.

We are in a position to pass to the limit
k\rightarrow\infty
. More precisely, in light of the estimates (
4.43
)-(
4.45
), (
4.56
) and (
4.60
)-(
4.61
), the Banach–Alaoglu theorem implies the existence of an octuple
(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w},p,q,\phi,\psi,\mu,\theta)
such that, up to a subsequence, as
k\rightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly-star in~}L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0,\textnormal{div}}),

(4.62)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly in~}L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{H}^{2})\cap H^{1}(0,T;\mathbfcal{L}^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\text{for any~}T>0,

(4.63)

\displaystyle(p_{k},q_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(p,q)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly in~}L^{2}(0,T;\mathcal{H}^{1}\cap\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(0)})

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\text{for any~}T>0,

(4.64)

\displaystyle(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(\phi,\psi)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly-star in~}L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{H}^{3}),

(4.65)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly in~}H^{1}(0,T;\mathcal{H}^{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\text{for any~}T>0,

(4.66)

\displaystyle(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(\mu,\theta)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly-star in~}L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{H}^{1}),

(4.67)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly in~}L^{2}(0,T;\mathcal{H}^{3})\cap H^{1}(0,T;(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\prime})

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\text{for any~}T>0.

(4.68)

As a consequence of the Aubin–Lions–Simon lemma, for any
T>0
, we further have

\displaystyle(\mathbf{v}_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}L^{2}(0,T;\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0,\textnormal{div}}),

(4.69)

\displaystyle(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(\phi,\psi)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}C([0,T];\mathcal{W}^{2,p})\ \text{for all~}p\in[2,\infty),

(4.70)

\displaystyle(\mu_{k},\theta_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow(\mu,\theta)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}L^{2}(0,T;\mathcal{H}^{2}).

(4.71)

Moreover, by Minty’s trick, we have

\displaystyle(F^{\prime}(\phi_{k}),G^{\prime}(\psi_{k}))\rightarrow(F^{\prime}(\phi),G^{\prime}(\psi))\hskip 20.00003pt\text{weakly-star in~}L^{\infty}(0,\infty;\mathcal{L}^{p})

for any
2\leq p<\infty
. Furthermore, on account of the assumptions on the coefficients (see
(A3)
-
(A4)
), we infer from (
4.70
) that

\displaystyle\rho(\phi_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow\rho(\phi),

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003ptm_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow m_{\Omega}(\phi),

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow\nu_{\Omega}(\phi)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}C(\overline{\Omega}\times[0,T]),

\displaystyle\sigma(\psi_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow\sigma(\psi),

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003ptm_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow m_{\Gamma}(\psi),

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}C(\Gamma\times[0,T]),

and

\displaystyle\gamma(\phi_{k},\psi_{k})

\displaystyle\rightarrow\gamma(\phi,\psi)\hskip 20.00003pt\text{strongly in~}C(\Gamma\times[0,T])

for any
T>0
.
The above properties entail the convergence of the nonlinear terms in (
4.3
), which allows us to pass to the limit as
k\rightarrow\infty
in (
4.3
), (
4.4
) and (
4.7
) (see, e.g.,
[
30
]
for the limit in the Galerkin formulation). Lastly, we observe that the pressure pair
(p,q)
satisfies

\displaystyle\nabla p=\rho(\phi)\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}+\rho(\phi)(\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}+(\mathbf{J}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}-\textnormal{div}(2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi)\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v})-\mu\nabla\phi\hskip 20.00003pt\text{a.e.~in~}Q

and

\displaystyle\nabla_{\Gamma}q

\displaystyle=\sigma(\psi)\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}+\sigma(\psi)(\mathbf{w}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}+(\mathbf{K}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}-\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi)\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w})-\theta\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi+2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi)[\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}\,\mathbf{n}]_{\tau}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt-\frac{1}{2}(\mathbf{J}\cdot\mathbf{n})\mathbf{w}+\frac{1}{2}(\mathbf{J}_{\Gamma}\cdot\mathbf{n})\mathbf{w}+\gamma(\phi,\psi)\mathbf{w}\hskip 20.00003pt\text{a.e.~in~}\Sigma,

which finishes the proof.
∎

5.
Uniqueness

In this section, we prove Theorem
3.4
. Namely, for
L\in(0,\infty]
, we show the uniqueness of strong solutions and their continuous dependence on the initial data.

Proof of Theorem
3.1
.

Let
(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{1},\mathbf{w}_{0}^{1},\phi_{0}^{1},\psi_{0}^{1})
and
(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{2},\mathbf{w}_{0}^{2},\phi_{0}^{2},\psi_{0}^{2})
be admissible initial data, and consider two global strong solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{1},\mathbf{w}_{1},p_{1},q_{1},\phi_{1},\psi_{1},\mu_{1},\theta_{1})
and
(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2},p_{2},q_{2},\phi_{2},\psi_{2},\mu_{2},\theta_{2})
originating from
(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{1},\mathbf{w}_{0}^{1},\phi_{0}^{1},\psi_{0}^{1})
and
(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{2},\mathbf{w}_{0}^{2},\phi_{0}^{2},\psi_{0}^{2})
, respectively. Set

\displaystyle(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w},p,q,\phi,\psi,\mu,\theta)\coloneqq(\mathbf{v}_{1}-\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{1}-\mathbf{w}_{2},p_{1}-p_{2},q_{1}-q_{2},\phi_{1}-\phi_{2},\psi_{1}-\psi_{2},\mu_{1}-\mu_{2},\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}).

It is clear that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\rho(\phi_{1})\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}+\big(\rho(\phi_{1})-\rho(\phi_{2})\big)\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{2}+\big(\rho(\phi_{1})(\mathbf{v}_{1}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{1}-\rho(\phi_{2})(\mathbf{v}_{2}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{2}\big)\\
&\qquad-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\big((m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\nabla\mu_{1}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{1}-(m_{\Omega}(\phi_{2})\nabla\mu_{2}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{2}\big)\\
&\qquad-\textnormal{div}(2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v})-\textnormal{div}\big(2(\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})-\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{2}))\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{2}\big)+\nabla p\\
&\quad=\mu_{1}\nabla\phi_{1}-\mu_{2}\nabla\phi_{2}\qquad\text{a.e.~in~}Q,\end{split}

(5.1a)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sigma(\psi_{1})\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}+\big(\sigma(\psi_{1})-\sigma(\psi_{2})\big)\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{2}+\big(\sigma(\psi_{1})(\mathbf{w}_{1}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{1}-\sigma(\psi_{2})(\mathbf{w}_{2}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{2}\big)\\
&\qquad-\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}\big((m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{1}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{1}-(m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{2})\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{2}\big)\\
&\qquad-\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w})-\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}\big(2(\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})-\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{2}))\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{2}\big)+\nabla_{\Gamma}q\\
&\quad=\theta_{1}\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi_{1}-\theta_{2}\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi_{2}-2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}\,\mathbf{n}-(2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})-2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{2}))\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{2}\,\mathbf{n}\\
&\qquad-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{1}\mathbf{w}_{1}+\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{2})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{2}\mathbf{w}_{2}\\
&\qquad+\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{1}\mathbf{w}_{1}-\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{2})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{2}\mathbf{w}_{2}\\
&\qquad-\gamma(\phi_{1},\psi_{1})\mathbf{w}_{1}+\gamma(\phi_{2},\psi_{2})\mathbf{w}_{2}\qquad\text{a.e.~on~}\Sigma,\end{split}

(5.1b)

as well as

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\phi+\mathbf{v}_{1}\cdot\nabla\phi+\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\phi_{2}=\textnormal{div}(m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\nabla\mu)+\textnormal{div}\big((m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})-m_{\Omega}(\phi_{2}))\nabla\mu_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\text{a.e.~in~}Q,

(5.1c)

\displaystyle\mu=-\Delta\phi+F^{\prime}(\phi_{1})-F^{\prime}(\phi_{2})

\displaystyle\text{a.e.~in~}Q,

(5.1d)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\psi+\mathbf{w}_{1}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi+\mathbf{w}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}\psi_{2}=\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta)+\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}\big((m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})-m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{2}))\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt-\beta m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu-\beta(m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})-m_{\Omega}(\phi_{2}))\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu_{2}

\displaystyle\text{a.e.~on~}\Sigma,

(5.1e)

\displaystyle\theta=-\Delta_{\Gamma}\psi+G^{\prime}(\psi_{1})-G^{\prime}(\psi_{2})+\alpha\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\phi

\displaystyle\text{a.e.~on~}\Sigma.

(5.1f)

Then, we multiply (
5.1a
) and (
5.1b
) by
\mathbf{v}
and
\mathbf{w}
, respectively, and integrate the respective equations over
\Omega
and
\Gamma
. Adding the resulting equations leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{1})|\mathbf{v}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{1})|\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{1},\psi_{1})|\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=-\int_{\Omega}\big(\rho(\phi_{1})-\rho(\phi_{2})\big)\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{v}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\big(\sigma(\psi_{1})-\sigma(\psi_{2})\big)\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{w}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{1})(\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{v}-\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{1})(\mathbf{w}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{w}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\int_{\Omega}\big(\rho(\phi_{1})-\rho(\phi_{2})\big)(\mathbf{v}_{2}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{v}\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\big(\sigma(\psi_{1})-\sigma(\psi_{2})\big)(\mathbf{w}_{2}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{w}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})(\nabla\mu\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{v}\;\mathrm{d}x+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})(\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{w}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\chi(L)}{2}\Big(\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\theta-\mu)\mathbf{w}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{w}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

(5.2)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\int_{\Omega}\big(m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})-m_{\Omega}(\phi_{2})\big)(\nabla\mu_{2}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{v}\;\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\big(m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})-m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{2})\big)(\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})\mathbf{w}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{w}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}2\big(\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})-\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{2})\big)\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{2}:\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\big(\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})-\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{2})\big)\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{2}:\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Gamma}\big(\gamma(\phi_{1},\psi_{1})-\gamma(\phi_{2},\psi_{2})\big)\mathbf{w}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{w}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\eqqcolon\sum_{j=1}^{14}W_{j}.

Here, we made use of

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\rho(\phi_{1})|\mathbf{v}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\sigma(\psi_{1})|\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{1})\mathbf{v}_{1}\cdot\nabla|\mathbf{v}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{1})\mathbf{w}_{1}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}|\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\nabla\mu_{1}\cdot\nabla|\mathbf{v}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{1}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}|\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\frac{\chi(L)}{2}\Big(\beta\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{1}-\tilde{\sigma}_{2}}{2}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{1}-\tilde{\rho}_{2}}{2}\Big)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\theta_{1}-\mu_{1})|\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=0,

which follows from the same considerations as in the proof of Theorem
3.1
(see also (
1.5
)). Next, noting on

\displaystyle Lm_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu=\beta\theta-\mu+\Big(1-\frac{m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})}{m_{\Omega}(\phi_{2})}\Big)\big(\beta\theta_{2}-\mu_{2})\hskip 20.00003pt\text{a.e.~on~}\Sigma

for
L>0
, a straightforward calculation shows in view of (
5.1c
)-(
5.1f
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}+\|(\mu,\theta)\|_{L,\beta,[\phi_{1},\psi_{1}]}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=-\big\langle(\partial_{t}\phi,\partial_{t}\psi),(F^{\prime}(\phi_{1})-F^{\prime}(\phi_{2}),G^{\prime}(\psi_{1})-G^{\prime}(\psi_{2}))\big\rangle_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-\int_{\Omega}\big(m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})-m_{\Omega}(\phi_{2})\big)\nabla\mu_{2}\cdot\nabla\mu\;\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\Gamma}\big(m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})-m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{2})\big)\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

(5.3)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}\big(\phi_{1}\mathbf{v}+\phi\mathbf{v}_{2}\big)\cdot\nabla\mu\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1}\mathbf{w}+\psi\mathbf{w}_{2}\big)\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\eqqcolon\sum_{j=15}^{19}W_{j}.

Hence, summing (
5
) and (
5
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{1})|\mathbf{v}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{1})|\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})|\nabla\mu|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})|\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\chi(L)\int_{\Gamma}(\beta\theta-\mu)^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}2\nu_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})|\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}2\nu_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})|\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\int_{\Gamma}\gamma(\phi_{1},\psi_{1})|\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt=\sum_{j=1}^{19}W_{j}.

To control the terms
W_{j}
,
j=1,\ldots,19
, we start by exploiting (
2.4
) as well as the regularity of strong solutions to obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|W_{1}+W_{2}|\begin{aligned} &\leq C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{6}}\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{2},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{3}}\\
&\leq\frac{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}+C\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{2},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2},\end{aligned}\end{split}

(5.4)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|W_{3}+W_{4}|\begin{aligned} &\leq C\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{3}}\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{6}}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}+C\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2},\end{aligned}\end{split}

(5.5)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|W_{5}+W_{6}|\begin{aligned} &\leq C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{6}}\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{6}}\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{6}}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\\
&\leq C\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}\big(\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\big).\end{aligned}\end{split}

(5.6)

Then, using additionally (
2.3
), we find that

\displaystyle|W_{7}+W_{8}+W_{9}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|(m_{\Omega}(\phi_{1})\nabla\mu,m_{\Gamma}(\psi_{1})\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta)\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+C\|\beta\theta-\mu\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{w}_{2}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}(\Gamma)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|(\mu,\theta)\|_{L,\beta,[\phi_{1},\psi_{1}]}^{2}+C\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+C\|\mathbf{w}_{2}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}(\Gamma)}^{2}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\mathbf{H}^{1}(\Gamma)}

(5.7)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|(\mu,\theta)\|_{L,\beta,[\phi_{1},\psi_{1}]}^{2}+\frac{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}+C\big(1+\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}\big)\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}|W_{10}+W_{11}|&\leq C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{6}}\|(\nabla\mu_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{6}}\|(\nabla\mathbf{v}_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{6}}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\\
&\leq C\big(\|(\nabla\mu_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}\big)\big(\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\big),\end{split}

(5.8)

and

\displaystyle|W_{12}+W_{13}+W_{14}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{6}}\|(\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{3}}\|(\mathbf{D}\mathbf{v},\mathbf{D}_{\Gamma}\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma)}+\|\psi\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma)}\big)\|\mathbf{w}_{2}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}(\Gamma)}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Gamma)}

(5.9)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}+C\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}.

Next,

\displaystyle|W_{15}|\leq\|(\partial_{t}\phi,\partial_{t}\psi)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})}\|(F^{\prime}(\phi_{1})-F^{\prime}(\phi_{2}),G^{\prime}(\psi_{1})-G^{\prime}(\psi_{2}))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}.

(5.10)

By means of a comparison argument in (
5.1c
)-(
5.1e
), we observe that

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{t}\phi,\partial_{t}\psi)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|(\zeta,\xi)\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}\leq 1}|\langle(\partial_{t}\phi,\partial_{t}\psi),(\zeta,\xi)\rangle_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}|

(5.11)

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{4}}\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}+\|(\mu,\theta)\|_{L,\beta,[\phi_{1},\psi_{1}]}+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{4}}\|(\nabla\mu_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}\big).

For the second term on the right-hand side of (
5.10
), we recall the existence of
\delta\in(0,1]
such that

\displaystyle|\phi_{j}|\leq 1-\delta\hskip 20.00003pt\text{on~}Q,\hskip 20.00003pt|\psi_{j}|\leq 1-\delta\hskip 20.00003pt\text{on~}\Sigma

(5.12)

for
j=1,2
. In particular, for any
s\in[0,1]
, it holds that

\displaystyle|s\phi_{1}+(1-s)\phi_{2}|\leq s(1-\delta)+(1-s)(1-\delta)=1-\delta\hskip 20.00003pt\text{on~}Q.

Similarly, we find that

\displaystyle|s\psi_{1}+(1-s)\psi_{2}|\leq 1-\delta\hskip 20.00003pt\text{on~}\Sigma

for any
s\in[0,1]
. Noting on the decomposition
F(s)=F_{0}(s)-\tfrac{c_{F}}{2}s^{2}
with
F_{0}\in C^{3}(-1,1)
, we use the fundamental theorem of calculus to infer

\displaystyle\|F^{\prime}(\phi_{1})-F^{\prime}(\phi_{2})\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq\int_{\Omega}|F_{0}^{\prime}(\phi_{1})-F_{0}^{\prime}(\phi_{2})|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Omega}|F_{0}^{\prime\prime}(\phi_{1})|^{2}|\nabla\phi|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Omega}|F_{0}^{\prime\prime}(\phi_{1})-F_{0}^{\prime\prime}(\phi_{2})|^{2}|\nabla\phi_{2}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+c_{F}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\phi^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+c_{F}^{2}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\phi|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq\int_{\Omega}\Big|\int_{0}^{1}F_{0}^{\prime\prime}(s\phi_{1}+(1-s)\phi_{2})^{2}\;\mathrm{d}s\Big|^{2}\phi^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+C\|\nabla\phi\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}

(5.13)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\int_{\Omega}\Big|\int_{0}^{1}F_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime}(s\phi_{1}+(1-s)\phi_{2})^{2}\;\mathrm{d}s\Big|^{2}\phi^{2}|\nabla\phi_{2}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+c_{F}^{2}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+c_{F}^{2}\|\nabla\phi\|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq\sup_{s\in[-1+\delta,1-\delta]}|F_{1}^{\prime\prime}(s)|^{2}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C\|\phi\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+\sup_{s\in[-1+\delta,1-\delta]}|F_{1}^{\prime\prime\prime}(s)|^{2}\|\phi\|_{L^{4}(\Omega)}^{2}\|\nabla\phi_{2}\|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}(\Omega)}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}.

Performing similar computations on the boundary, we deduce

\displaystyle\|G^{\prime}(\psi_{1})-G^{\prime}(\psi_{2})\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma)}^{2}\leq C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}.

(5.14)

Combining (
5
) and (
5.14
) leads to

\displaystyle\|(F^{\prime}(\phi_{1})-F^{\prime}(\phi_{2}),G^{\prime}(\psi_{1})-G^{\prime}(\psi_{2}))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}\leq C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}.

(5.15)

Thus, going back to (
5.10
), the estimates (
5
) and (
5.15
) entail that

\displaystyle|W_{15}|

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{4}}\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}+\|(\mu,\theta)\|_{L,\beta,[\phi_{1},\psi_{1}]}+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{4}}\|(\nabla\mu_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\times C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}

(5.16)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\mu,\theta)\|_{L,\beta}^{2}+C\big(1+\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mu_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}}^{2}\big)\big(\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\big).

Lastly, for the remaining terms, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\begin{aligned} |W_{16}+W_{17}|&\leq C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{4}}\|(\nabla\mu_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}\|(\nabla\mu,\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta)\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\mu,\theta)\|_{L,\beta}^{2}+C\|(\nabla\mu_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}}^{2}\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2},\end{aligned}\end{split}

(5.17)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\begin{aligned} |W_{18}+W_{19}|&\leq\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\|(\nabla\mu,\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta)\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}+C\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{4}}\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{4}}\|(\nabla\mu,\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta)\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}}{8}\|(\mu,\theta)\|_{L,\beta}^{2}+C\big(1+\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}}^{2}\big)\big(\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\big).\end{aligned}\end{split}

(5.18)

Therefore, by (
5.4
)-(
5
) and (
5
)-(
5.18
), we obtain the differential inequality

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Omega}\rho(\phi_{1})|\mathbf{v}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\Gamma}\sigma(\psi_{1})|\mathbf{w}|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\Gamma+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\Big)+\frac{\min\{1,m_{\ast}\}}{2}\|(\mu,\theta)\|_{L,\beta}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\frac{\min\{2\nu_{\ast},\gamma_{\ast}\}}{2}\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}_{0}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq C\big(1+\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{2},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{2},\mathbf{w}_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mu_{2},\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2})\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}}^{2}\big)\big(\|(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\phi,\psi)\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\big).

Noting on
\rho\geq\rho_{\ast}>0
a.e. on
Q
and
\sigma\geq\sigma_{\ast}>0
a.e. on
\Sigma
, we deduce with the help of the Gronwall lemma that

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{v}(t),\mathbf{w}(t))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}+\|(\phi(t),\psi(t))\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\leq C\big(\|(\mathbf{v}(0),\mathbf{w}(0))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\phi(0),\psi(0))\|_{K,\alpha}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\times\exp\Big(\int_{0}^{T}1+\|(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{2}(\tau),\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{2}(\tau))\|_{\mathbfcal{L}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{2}(\tau),\mathbf{w}_{2}(\tau))\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mu_{2}(\tau),\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta_{2}(\tau))\|_{\mathbfcal{H}^{1}}^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\tau\Big)

for all
t\in[0,T]
for any
T>0
, which implies the uniqueness of strong solutions.
∎

Appendix A
The convective bulk-surface Cahn–Hilliard equation with non-degenerate mobility

In this section, we study the following convective bulk-surface Cahn–Hilliard equation with non-degenerate mobility:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\phi+\textnormal{div}(\phi\mathbf{v})=\textnormal{div}(m_{\Omega}(\phi)\nabla\mu)

\displaystyle\text{in}\ Q,

(A.1a)

\displaystyle\mu=-\Delta\phi+F^{\prime}(\phi)

\displaystyle\text{in}\ Q,

(A.1b)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\psi+\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(\psi\mathbf{w})=\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}(m_{\Gamma}(\psi)\nabla_{\Gamma}\theta)-\beta m_{\Omega}(\phi)\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu

\displaystyle\text{on}\ \Sigma,

(A.1c)

\displaystyle\theta=-\Delta_{\Gamma}\psi+G^{\prime}(\psi)+\alpha\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\phi

\displaystyle\text{on}\ \Sigma,

(A.1d)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}K\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\phi=\alpha\psi-\phi&\text{if}\ K\in[0,\infty),\\
\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\phi=0&\text{if}\ K=\infty\end{cases}

\displaystyle\text{on}\ \Sigma,

(A.1e)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}Lm_{\Omega}(\phi)\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu=\beta\theta-\mu&\text{if}\ L\in[0,\infty),\\
m_{\Omega}(\phi)\partial_{\mathbf{n}}\mu=0&\text{if}\ L=\infty\end{cases}

\displaystyle\text{on}\ \Sigma,

(A.1f)

\displaystyle\phi|_{t=0}=\phi_{0}

\displaystyle\text{in}\ \Omega,

(A.1g)

\displaystyle\psi|_{t=0}=\psi_{0}

\displaystyle\text{on}\ \Gamma,

(A.1h)

where the mobility functions
m_{\Omega}
and
m_{\Gamma}
are supposed to satisfy the assumptions as stated in
(A4)
. Moreover, the prescribed velocity fields are such that
(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w})\in L^{2}(0,\infty;\mathbfcal{L}^{2}_{\textnormal{div}})
. The system (
A.1
) can be regarded as a subsystem of the full Navier–Stokes–Cahn–Hilliard system (
1.1
). For constant mobility functions, the well-posedness of strong solutions to (
A.1
